```python
def _drum_warned(condition: str, index: int, owed: int) -> str:
    """The changes feed's warning at a drummed trial's tenth failed try in a row (the PI,
    2026-10-12, "After 10 in a row"): drumming goes on. *Clear one* moves past the trial once the
    training aid is switched on (the PI, 2026-10-18) -- while its condition owes a trial; past a
    criterion block's targets it owes none, a clear there is refused, and only *stop drumming*
    ends the drum (the controller's ruling M3 on the plan's revision review)."""
    way = ("clear one moves past it once clear is switched on, stop drumming ends it" if owed
           else "stop drumming ends it")
    return (f"{condition} has failed {DRUM_WARNING} tries in a row under drumming, the last trial "
            f"{index}; drumming goes on: {way}")
```

- `train`'s `if setting not in TRAINING:` → `if False:`: the same test fails (no `ValueError`).
- `aids`' `("clear", self.clearing)` → `("clear", False)`: `test_each_draw_names_the_training_aids_on_as_it_was_drawn` fails; its `("drumming", self.drumming is not None)` → `("drumming", False)`: the same test fails, and `test_a_blocks_drumming_works_in_a_piloting_session_and_its_rows_mark_it`.
- `next_trial`'s `aids=self.aids,` → `aids=(),`: `test_each_draw_names_the_training_aids_on_as_it_was_drawn` fails.
- The taskd row's `aids=drawn.aids,` → `aids=(),`: `test_clear_is_refused_until_a_trainer_switches_it_on_and_each_row_marks_the_aids_on` fails.
- `Session._command` without the `Training` branch: the same test fails (refused as a command the session does not act on).
- `Session._train`'s `was=was,` → `was=None,`: the same test fails; its `if was == command.on:` → `if False:` (a second switch recorded): the same test fails.
- `_drum_warned`'s `if owed` → `if True` (*clear one* named where nothing is owed): `test_the_drum_warning_names_only_stop_drumming_where_the_condition_owes_nothing` fails.
- `_encode_command`'s `"on": command.on}` → `"on": True}`: `test_a_training_switch_survives_the_wire_with_who_sent_it[command1]` fails.
- `_command_from`'s `if data.get("setting") not in TRAINING:` → `if False:`: `test_a_malformed_training_switch_is_refused_where_it_is_decoded[fields0-…]` and `[fields1-…]` fail; its `if not isinstance(data.get("on"), bool):` → `if False:`: `…[fields2-…]` fails.

Body: the PI's decisions 10 to 12 of 2026-10-18, quoted; the drum's warning naming only *stop drumming* where nothing is owed (ruling M3); a switch to the value already set refused (the smaller ruling); `Counting.cap`'s docstring (ruling M1); drumming already off until declared or switched, now marked; *clear*'s refusal while off a control not switched on; each switch for the run it is made in (Appendix B, item 18); the switch's branch in `Session._command`, beside the pause and Task 8's two, outside its listed parts (Appendix A, item 4); the drum's warning's sentence; the red an import error by design; the count; the breaks seen failing.

---

### Task 15: A trial and its drummed repeats are one trial: each condition's counts, kept for the run's end row, and the strip's tallies of drummed tries

The PI's decisions 5, 15 and 16 of 2026-10-18:

- **"200 trials; drummed repeats inside them"** (asked: 'In a training run set to "200 trials" with drumming on, what is 200?') -- "A trial and its drummed repeats are one of the 200 ... The console would show both: trials (200) and presentations (e.g. 260)." Every presentation keeps its own line and trial number in the record. **Built** for what a run pays (under drumming only the right try pays, so "run N trials" is N trials, each ending right) and for the record; **not built**: any count of trials as against presentations.
- **"Show both"** (asked about the strip's percent correct under drumming): "two percentages side by side: right first time (per trial, a trial and its drummed repeats one) and right per presentation (every try)".
- **Per condition, all four** (asked what the console shows per condition): trials and presentations; right, wrong, and percent right first time; owed, repeats placed, and moved past, with the possible-avoidance flag; drummed tries, and the current failed run. "All of these are also written into the run's end record."

Built here, in the engine (the console is Task 9's): **each condition's counters gain** its `trials` (a drum's repeat is none), every answer `right` or `wrong` on any try (Task 12's answers), `right_first` (the trials whose first try was right) and whether its limit `flagged` it; **`ConditionReport`**, one condition's counts in one block, and `Scheduler.report`, every block's so far -- the scheduler kept a block's counts only until it advanced -- which **the run's end row records as `conditions`** (the brief put the end row under Task 11; it is code, so it is built and tested here, and Task 11 records it). **The strip's tallies** (`simulate.Tally`, at the session, task, run and block) **count each level's drummed tries and the right ones among them**, from which Task 9 shows right first time beside right per presentation, and a resumed session rebuilds them from each line's `drummed`.

**Amended 2026-10-18 by the controller's rulings on the revision's review**: the run counts its trials too (`Scheduler.trials`, ruling I1: the console's trial count is trials, Task 9); **the run's end row's `trials` is that count**, a trial and its drummed repeats one, and it gains **`presentations`**, its lines (`self._index`) -- nothing reads the end row's `trials` today (wl-preproc's `rigruns.py` reads `stopped_because` and `stop_kind`; wl-xcon's `resume.py` reads start rows), and before C2a the two were equal (ruling M5); **each condition's `presentations` and `trials` are counted as each try is recorded**, so a trial that faulted, which has no line, is in neither the end row's figures nor its condition's (ruling M5); and **`failing` is written to the end row's `conditions` too** (ruling M6: decision 16, "all of them"). A break a hold-training block's own table scores (decision 17, Task 12) counts as one of its condition's wrong answers, as its rule judges it.

**What "trials" counts** (Appendix B, item 19): a drawn trial that is not a drum's repeat. An ordinary repeat -- a break shown again a few trials later -- is a trial of its own, as it always was: decision 5 is about drumming. **What does not change** (Appendix B, item 20): each line's `index` and trial number, the run's end row's `trials`, telemetry's `trial_index` and a scheduled "stop after N trials" all still count presentations; the last is welfare-listed (`Session._ends`, `_schedule`), so it is not touched here, and stopping sooner is the conservative side.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/scheduler.py` (`Counts`; `ConditionReport`, before `Scheduler`; `Scheduler._reports`, `trials`; `_report`, `block_report` and `report`, before `counts`; `advance`; `record`; `_forgive`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/simulate.py` (imports; `Tally.drummed`, `right_drummed`; `Tally.add`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/levels.py` (`Levels.end_trial`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/resume.py` (imports; the restored tallies)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (the tallies; the run's end row's `trials`, `presentations` and `conditions`)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_scheduler.py` (imports; two new), `tests/test_levels.py` (one new), `tests/test_resume.py` (one new), `tests/test_taskd.py` (imports; three new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scheduler._answer`, `drum_tries`, `_drum` (Tasks 6, 12); `Draw.drummed` (Task 6).
- Produces: `Counts.trials`, `presented`, `right`, `wrong`, `right_first`, `flagged`; `Scheduler.trials`, the run's; `scheduler.ConditionReport(block, condition, trials, presentations, right, wrong, right_first, owed, repeats, moved_past, possible_avoidance, drummed, failing)`, frozen; `Scheduler.block_report` and `report`, tuples of them; `Tally.add(result, drummed=False)`, `Tally.drummed`, `right_drummed`; `Levels.end_trial(result, drummed=False)`; the run's end row's `trials` (the run's trials) and `presentations` (its lines), and its `conditions`, a list of `{"block", "condition", "trials", "presentations", "right", "wrong", "right_first", "owed", "repeats", "moved_past", "possible_avoidance", "drummed", "failing"}`.

```python
def test_a_conditions_report_counts_a_drummed_trial_once_and_every_try_as_a_presentation():
    """The PI, 2026-10-18: "A trial and its drummed repeats are one" trial, and per condition the
    console shows "trials and presentations; right, wrong, % right (first time); owed, repeats,
    moved past ...; drummed tries (and the current failed run)". Three trials, two of them drummed
    to the right answer, are five presentations; one was right first time."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=3)], drum=True)],
                          seed=1)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    failing = scheduler.block_report[0].failing
    for outcome in (Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcome)

    assert failing == 1 and scheduler.trials == 3
    assert scheduler.block_report == (
        ConditionReport("b", "a", trials=3, presentations=5, right=3, wrong=1, right_first=1,
                        owed=0, repeats=0, moved_past=0, possible_avoidance=False, drummed=2,
                        failing=0),)


def test_the_report_keeps_every_blocks_counts_and_flags_a_condition_its_limit_moved_past():
    """The PI, 2026-10-18: each condition's counts are "written into the run's end record as well",
    every block's -- the scheduler kept a block's counts only until it advanced -- with the
    possible-avoidance flag where a repeat limit moved the condition past."""
    blocks = [Block(name="A", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=2)],
                    counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, cap=0)),
              Block(name="B", conditions=[Condition("b", {}, target=1)])]
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks, seed=1)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)
    assert scheduler.finished

    scheduler.advance()
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert [(r.block, r.condition, r.trials, r.moved_past, r.possible_avoidance, r.right)
            for r in scheduler.report] == [("A", "a", 1, 2, True, 0), ("B", "b", 1, 0, False, 1)]
```

```python
def test_a_runs_end_row_records_each_conditions_counts_a_drummed_trial_one_trial(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-18: "200 trials; drummed repeats inside them", and each condition's trials
    and presentations, right and wrong answers and right first time, what it owes, its repeats, the
    trials moved past and its drummed tries are "written into the run's end record as well". The
    end row's `trials` counts trials and its `presentations` its lines (the controller's ruling
    M5). The detection task withholds, so a miss is a wrong answer."""
    block = Block(name="drill", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=20)], drum=True)
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]))

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    (end,) = [row for row in _runs(session) if row["event"] == "end"]
    (counts,) = end["conditions"]
    wrong = {o.value for o in ANSWERS - RIGHT} | {"no_response"}
    assert (counts["block"], counts["condition"], counts["trials"], counts["owed"]) == (
        "drill", "c", 20, 0)
    assert counts["presentations"] == len(rows) == end["presentations"]
    assert end["trials"] == 20 and counts["failing"] == 0
    assert counts["drummed"] == sum(row["drummed"] for row in rows) > 0
    assert (counts["right"], counts["wrong"]) == (20, sum(row["outcome"] in wrong for row in rows))
    assert counts["right_first"] == sum(row["outcome"] == "correct" and not row["drummed"]
                                        for row in rows)
    assert (counts["repeats"], counts["moved_past"], counts["possible_avoidance"]) == (0, 0, False)


def test_a_trial_that_faults_is_in_neither_the_end_rows_counts_nor_its_conditions(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The controller's ruling M5: a trial that faulted has no line, so it is counted in neither
    the end row's `presentations` and `trials` nor its condition's. Here a trial's first try and
    three drummed tries fail, and the fifth try faults."""
    from wl_xcon import taskd

    real, calls = taskd.run_trial, []

    def wrong_then_a_fault(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append(None)
        if len(calls) == 5:
            raise RuntimeError("the card stopped answering")
        return dataclasses.replace(real(*args, **kwargs), outcome=Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)

    monkeypatch.setattr(taskd, "run_trial", wrong_then_a_fault)
    block = Block(name="drill", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=20)], drum=True)
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]))

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="the card stopped answering"):
        session.run()

    (end,) = [row for row in _runs(session) if row["event"] == "end"]
    (counts,) = end["conditions"]
    assert (end["stop_kind"], len(_trial_rows(session))) == ("fault", 4)
    assert (end["trials"], end["presentations"]) == (1, 4)
    assert (counts["trials"], counts["presentations"], counts["drummed"]) == (1, 4, 3)


def test_a_drum_failing_as_its_run_is_stopped_is_on_the_end_row(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The controller's ruling M6: `failing`, the failed tries in a row of a trial being drummed,
    is on the end row's `conditions` too (decision 16: "all of them"): a run a person stops while
    a trial has failed five tries."""
    from wl_xcon import taskd

    real = taskd.run_trial

    def wrong(*args, **kwargs):
        return dataclasses.replace(real(*args, **kwargs), outcome=Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)

    monkeypatch.setattr(taskd, "run_trial", wrong)
    block = Block(name="drill", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=20)], drum=True)
    link = _Drained({6: [Stop(by=Box("jake"))]})
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]), link=link)

    session.run()

    (end,) = [row for row in _runs(session) if row["event"] == "end"]
    (counts,) = end["conditions"]
    assert (end["stop_kind"], end["trials"], end["presentations"]) == ("operator", 1, 5)
    assert (counts["failing"], counts["drummed"]) == (5, 4)
```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ConditionReport:
    """One condition's counts in one block of the run, for the console and the run's end row (the
    PI, 2026-10-18, decisions 15 and 16): its `trials`, a trial and its drummed repeats one, and its
    `presentations`, every try; its answers `right` and `wrong`, every try's, and `right_first`,
    the trials whose first try was right; what it still `owed`, the ordinary `repeats` placed, the
    trials `moved_past` -- forgiven by its repeat limit or a person's clear -- and whether its limit
    flagged it as `possible_avoidance`; its `drummed` tries, and `failing`, the failed tries in a
    row of its trial being drummed now."""

    block: str
    condition: str
    trials: int
    presentations: int
    right: int
    wrong: int
    right_first: int
    owed: int
    repeats: int
    moved_past: int
    possible_avoidance: bool
    drummed: int
    failing: int
```

```python
    def _report(self, condition: str) -> ConditionReport:
        counts = self._counts[condition]
        waiting = self._drum is not None and self._drum[0] == condition
        return ConditionReport(
            self.block.name, condition, counts.trials, counts.presented, counts.right,
            counts.wrong, counts.right_first, self.owed(condition), counts.repeats,
            counts.forgiven, counts.flagged, counts.presented - counts.trials,
            self.drum_tries if waiting else 0,
        )

    @property
    def block_report(self) -> tuple:
        """The open block's `ConditionReport`s, one per condition in its order: the console's."""
        return tuple(self._report(c.name) for c in self.block.conditions)

    @property
    def report(self) -> tuple:
        """Every block's `ConditionReport`s so far, in order, the open one's last: what the run's
        end row records (the PI, 2026-10-18: "written into the run's end record as well")."""
        return tuple(self._reports) + self.block_report
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them and see them pass**

Run the four files: `458 passed`. Then the whole suite: **3736 passed**.

- [ ] **Step 5: Prove they can fail**

- `record`'s `counts.trials += first_try` → `counts.trials += 1` (every try a new trial, as built): `test_a_conditions_report_counts_a_drummed_trial_once_and_every_try_as_a_presentation` fails, and `test_a_runs_end_row_records_each_conditions_counts_a_drummed_trial_one_trial`.
- `record`'s `counts.wrong += outcome not in RIGHT` → `counts.wrong += 0`: the same scheduler test fails; its `counts.right_first += first_try and outcome in RIGHT` → `counts.right_first += outcome in RIGHT`: the same test fails.
- `_report`'s `self.drum_tries if waiting else 0,` → `0,`: the same test fails (`failing`).
- `advance` without `self._reports.extend(self.block_report)`: `test_the_report_keeps_every_blocks_counts_and_flags_a_condition_its_limit_moved_past` fails; `_forgive`'s `self._counts[condition].flagged |= because == CAP` → `pass`: the same test fails.
- `Tally.add`'s `self.right_drummed += result.outcome in RIGHT` → `self.right_drummed += 0`: `test_a_drummed_try_is_counted_at_every_level_with_the_right_ones_among_them` fails; `Levels.end_trial`'s `self.block_tally.add(result, drummed)` → `self.block_tally.add(result)`: the same test fails.
- `resume`'s `if line.get("drummed"):` → `if False:`: `test_a_restored_tally_counts_each_drummed_try_and_the_right_ones_among_them` fails.
- The end row's `"right_first": report.right_first,` → `"right_first": report.right,`: `test_a_runs_end_row_records_each_conditions_counts_a_drummed_trial_one_trial` fails; its `trials=scheduler.trials,` → `trials=self._index,` (presentations, as built): the same test fails; `record`'s `self.trials += first_try` → `self.trials += 1`: the same test fails.
- `_report`'s `counts.trials, counts.presented, counts.right,` → `counts.trials, counts.attempted, counts.right,` (draws, a faulted trial among them): `test_a_trial_that_faults_is_in_neither_the_end_rows_counts_nor_its_conditions` fails; the end row's `"failing": report.failing,` → `"failing": 0,`: `test_a_drum_failing_as_its_run_is_stopped_is_on_the_end_row` fails.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add wl_xcon/scheduler.py wl_xcon/simulate.py wl_xcon/levels.py wl_xcon/resume.py wl_xcon/taskd.py tests/test_scheduler.py tests/test_levels.py tests/test_resume.py tests/test_taskd.py
git commit -m "Count a trial and its drummed repeats as one: each condition's counts on the run's end row, and drummed tries at every level"
```
Body: the PI's decisions 5, 15 and 16 of 2026-10-18, quoted; the end row's `trials` and `presentations`, a faulted trial in neither, and `failing` on its `conditions` (the controller's rulings I1, M5 and M6); what "trials" counts and what still counts presentations (Appendix B, items 19 and 20; the scheduled stop welfare-listed); the end row's `conditions`, every block's; the strip's tallies for Task 9; the red an import error by design; the count; the breaks seen failing.

---

### Task 9: The console shows each condition's counts, what is forgiven and drummed, the training aids and both percentages, and offers the controls

Engine spec §16.6: the shortfall "recorded, shown and flagged as possible avoidance"; the scope's item 14, "owed for every condition of the open block" (`Telemetry.owed` named only the queued conditions, one under a block drawing with replacement); and the page's controls for Task 8's commands. Telemetry schema 16 (call 13; the next number if the warnings build took 16, the header says). The page's Still-needed pane lists every condition with what it owes and, beside each owing any while a run runs, *clear one*, and *clear the rest* where it owes more than one -- **only where a trial of the open block can be owed** (`Telemetry.clearable`: the engineering review's Minor 3; a plain "run N trials" owes nothing unless drummed, and a clear there would only shorten it); then each shortfall, a repeat limit's flagged as possible avoidance or named for the rig, a clear's with who cleared it; then drumming and the switches that change it. `wlx serve` builds both commands from a page's body by the wire's own function, as it builds the service's.

**Revised 2026-10-18 for the PI's decisions of that day** (Tasks 12 to 15 build them in the engine; this task shows them):
- **Two percentages** (decision 15, "Show both": "right first time (per trial, a trial and its drummed repeats one) and right per presentation (every try)"): the strip's line at each level leads with right first time wherever trials were drummed there, and its note gives right per presentation; with nothing drummed the two are one figure, shown as before. Each level's counts carry its drummed tries and the right ones among them (`Counts.drummed`, `right_drummed`; Task 15's tallies).
- **Per condition** (decision 16): beneath each condition's line in the Still-needed pane, its trials and presentations, right and wrong answers and percent right first time, repeats placed, trials moved past with the possible-avoidance flag, drummed tries, and the failed run of its trial being drummed now (`Telemetry.conditions`, `ConditionRow`s from Task 15's `Scheduler.block_report`). What it owes stays on its line, as built; each shortfall stays below, as built.
- **Trials, and presentations beside them** (decision 5, as the controller's ruling I1 on the revision's review keeps it): the run's trial count the page shows -- the Working? pane's big number -- and /health's "Trials" count trials, a trial and its drummed repeats one (`Telemetry.trials`, from Task 15's `Scheduler.trials`), with the presentations beside them where they differ ("20 trials · 25 presentations"; /health's "Presentations"). What still counts presentations says so: the recent outcomes' ticks ("the last 60 of M presentations": they list lines), the strip's rate ("presentations/min") and the scheduled stop's option ("after N more presentations, drummed tries included" -- the PI kept it counting presentations on 2026-10-18; page text only, `_schedule` and `_ends` untouched).
- **The training aids** (decisions 10 to 12): a **standing banner while drumming or *clear* is on** and the run goes -- in a training session "drumming and clear on: the record marks every trial"; in a piloting or recording one a warning naming the session's kind and that training aids are meant for training sessions ("Allow it, with a warning") -- and, while a run runs, **the training switches** beside the drumming ones: *clear* on or off, and how a percentage counts drummed answers (`link.Training`, Task 14). ***Clear one* and *clear the rest* are offered only while *clear* is on** (`Telemetry.clearing`), and still only where a trial of the block can be owed (`clearable`). `wlx serve` builds the training switch as it builds *clear* and *drum*. The banner reads the scheduler's own list of the aids on (`Telemetry.aids`), never recomputing it (the controller's smaller ruling). **Where a switch changes nothing** (ruling M4): with *clear* on where nothing in the run can be cleared, the pane says so; the first-answers switch, in a block whose rule is a streak, says it changes nothing there (`Telemetry.streak`). **Past a criterion block's targets** the pane says it is drawing until the criterion is met (`Telemetry.past_targets`; the smaller ruling).

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/scheduler.py` (`Scheduler.clearable`, before `clear`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/link.py` (imports; `SCHEMA` 16 and its history line; `Counts.drummed`, `right_drummed`; `ShortfallRow`, `ConditionRow`; `Telemetry.owed`'s comment, `shortfalls`, `drumming`, `clearable`, `clearing`, `first_answers`, `conditions`; `Telemetry.of`; `_counts_out`, `_counts_in`; `_telemetry_out`; `_telemetry_from`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (`_counts`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/health.py` (`readings`: Trials, and Presentations beside)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/serve.py` (`_SHAPES`; `_WIRE_KINDS`; `parse_command`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/web.py` (imports; `_performance`'s rate; `_trials`; `_work`; the scheduled stop's option; `_level`; `_aids_banner`, `_banners`; `DRUM_SWITCHES`, `_FORGIVEN`, `_counted`, `_need`; `fragments`; the page's click handler)
- Modify (test): `tests/_frames.py` and `tests/test_cli.py` (each full frame gains the six fields), `tests/test_link.py` (imports; the schema pins move to 16; three new), `tests/test_serve.py` (imports; four cases; one new), `tests/test_web.py` (imports; eight new; the escaping test's frames), `tests/test_page_browser.py` (imports; one new), `tests/test_taskd.py` (two new), `tests/test_health.py` (one changed; one new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scheduler.shortfalls`, `Shortfall` (Tasks 7, 8); `Scheduler.drumming` (Task 6); `ClearOwed`, `Drum` (Task 8); `Scheduler.clearing`, `first_answers`, `streak`, `past_targets`, `aids`, `trials`, `block_report`, `ConditionReport`, `link.Training` (Tasks 12 to 15); `Tally.drummed`, `right_drummed` (Task 15).
- Produces: `Scheduler.clearable -> bool`; `link.SCHEMA = 16`; `link.ShortfallRow(block, condition, trials, because, possible_avoidance, by, after_trial)`, frozen; `link.ConditionRow(condition, trials, presentations, right, wrong, right_first, owed, repeats, moved_past, possible_avoidance, drummed, failing)`, frozen; `link.Counts.drummed`, `right_drummed`, 0 by default; `Telemetry.shortfalls: tuple` of `ShortfallRow`s, `Telemetry.drumming: str | None`, `Telemetry.clearable: bool`, `Telemetry.clearing: bool`, `Telemetry.first_answers: bool`, `Telemetry.conditions: tuple` of `ConditionRow`s, `Telemetry.trials: int`, `Telemetry.aids: tuple`, `Telemetry.streak: bool` and `Telemetry.past_targets: bool`, all required, after `warnings`; `Telemetry.owed` keyed by every condition of the open block in its order, zero included; `web.DRUM_SWITCHES`; `web._need(frame, view)`; `serve._WIRE_KINDS`. The page's buttons: `data-cmd="clear" data-condition="<name>"` (with `data-rest="1"` for *clear the rest*), `data-cmd="drum" data-scope="block|run|off"` and `data-cmd="training" data-setting="clear|first_answers" data-on="1|0"`.

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Schema 16 (engine build C2a): what is still owed, forgiven and drummed
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_schema_16_carries_each_shortfall_drumming_the_training_aids_and_each_conditions_counts():
    """Engine spec §16.6; the PI, 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-18: a cap's shortfall flagged as possible
    avoidance, a cleared one with who cleared it, whether trials are drummed, the training aid
    *clear* and the percentage setting, each condition's counts, and each level's drummed tries
    with the right ones among them. A reader of 16 refuses 15."""
    sent = _telemetry(
        shortfalls=(ShortfallRow("b", "near", 3, "cap", True, None, 12),
                    ShortfallRow("b", "far", 1, "cleared", False, Box("jake"), None)),
        drumming="run",
        clearable=False,
        clearing=True,
        first_answers=True,
        conditions=(ConditionRow("near", 12, 15, 9, 4, 8, 2, 3, 1, True, 3, 2),),
        trials=12,
        aids=("drumming", "clear"),
        streak=True,
        past_targets=True,
    )
    sent = replace(sent, performance=replace(
        sent.performance, session=Counts({"correct": 9}, 0, drummed=3, right_drummed=2)))

    assert decode(encode(sent)) == sent
    with pytest.raises(SchemaMismatch, match="carried schema 15 and this console reads schema 16"):
        decode(encode(replace(sent, schema=15)))


def test_a_frame_names_every_condition_of_the_open_block_and_its_scheduler_s_shortfalls():
    """Engine spec §16.6: "owed" for every condition of the open block, a condition owing nothing
    included -- it named only those queued, one under a block drawing with replacement -- and the
    run's shortfalls and drumming, the training aid *clear* and the percentage setting, and each
    condition's counts (the PI, 2026-10-18), read from the scheduler."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("near", {}, target=1),
                                        Condition("far", {}, target=2)], drum=True)
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks=[block], seed=0, clearing=True)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    paid = scheduler.drawn.condition.name
    other = "far" if paid == "near" else "near"
    scheduler.clear(other, by=Box("jake"))

    frame = Telemetry.of(_session_with(delivered_ml=1.0, already_today=None), Tally(), scheduler,
                         index=1)

    assert list(frame.owed) == ["near", "far"]
    assert frame.owed[paid] == {"near": 0, "far": 1}[paid]
    assert frame.owed[other] == {"near": 0, "far": 1}[other]
    assert frame.shortfalls == (ShortfallRow("b", other, 1, "cleared", False, Box("jake"), 0),)
    assert (frame.drumming, frame.clearing, frame.first_answers) == ("block", True, False)
    assert (frame.trials, frame.aids, frame.streak, frame.past_targets) == (
        1, ("drumming", "clear"), False, False)
    assert [(row.condition, row.trials, row.right, row.owed, row.moved_past)
            for row in frame.conditions] == [
        (name, int(name == paid), int(name == paid), frame.owed[name], int(name == other))
        for name in ("near", "far")]


def test_a_frame_says_whether_a_trial_of_the_open_block_can_be_cleared():
    """The engineering review's Minor 3: a plain run of N, whose every-trial table owes nothing,
    offers no *clear* -- one would only shorten the run -- until its trials are drummed."""
    flat = Scheduler([Block(name="session", conditions=[Condition("session", {}, target=5)],
                            counting=EVERY_TRIAL)])
    owing = Scheduler([Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("near", {}, target=5)])],
                      counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED))

    def clearable(scheduler) -> bool:
        session = _session_with(delivered_ml=1.0, already_today=None)
        return Telemetry.of(session, Tally(), scheduler, index=1).clearable

    assert (clearable(flat), clearable(owing)) == (False, True)
    flat.drum("run")
    assert clearable(flat)


def test_a_frame_says_a_criterion_block_is_past_its_targets_and_whether_its_rule_is_a_streak():
    """The controller's rulings on the plan's revision review: past a criterion block's targets the
    page says it draws until the criterion is met, and in a block whose rule is a streak the
    first-answers switch says it changes nothing (M4); neither once the criterion is met."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("near", {}, target=1)], criterion=(1.0, 2))
    scheduler = Scheduler([block])

    def frame() -> Telemetry:
        session = _session_with(delivered_ml=1.0, already_today=None)
        return Telemetry.of(session, Tally(), scheduler, index=1)

    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert (frame().past_targets, frame().streak) == (True, True)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.finished and not frame().past_targets
```

```python
def test_still_needed_offers_to_clear_a_trial_of_each_condition_owing_one_while_a_run_runs():
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: "some way in the console to manually advance beyond or clear a specific
    trial in the count"; *clear the rest* beside it where a condition owes more than one (the
    science review's D5), while the training aid *clear* is on (the PI, 2026-10-18). A condition
    owing nothing has no button; nothing is offered once the run has ended, and away from where
    controls work each is greyed."""
    need = fragments(frame(owed={"near": 2, "far": 0, "mid": 1}, clearing=True), view())["rt-need"]
    ended = fragments(frame(owed={"near": 2}, stop_kind="completed", clearing=True),
                      view())["rt-need"]
    lan = fragments(frame(owed={"near": 2}, clearing=True), view(can_write=False))["rt-need"]

    one = '<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="clear" data-condition="near">clear one'
    assert one in need
    assert 'data-cmd="clear" data-condition="near" data-rest="1">clear the rest' in need
    assert 'data-condition="far"' not in need
    assert 'data-condition="mid">clear one' in need
    assert 'data-condition="mid" data-rest' not in need
    assert 'data-cmd="clear"' not in ended and 'data-cmd="drum"' not in ended
    assert 'data-cmd="training"' not in ended
    assert 'data-cmd="clear" data-condition="near" disabled' in lan


def test_still_needed_offers_no_clear_where_nothing_of_the_block_can_be_owed():
    """The engineering review's Minor 3: a plain "run N trials", whose every trial pays, owes
    nothing to move past, so *clear one* would only shorten it; the counts and the drumming
    switches stay."""
    need = fragments(frame(owed={"session": 30}, clearable=False, clearing=True), view())["rt-need"]

    assert 'data-cmd="clear"' not in need and '<span class="num">30</span>' in need
    assert 'data-cmd="drum"' in need


def test_still_needed_shows_each_shortfall_a_caps_flagged_as_possible_avoidance():
    """Engine spec §16.6: "its shortfall recorded, shown and flagged as possible avoidance"; one
    the rig made is named for the rig and not flagged (the engineering review's M4); a cleared one
    says who cleared it."""
    need = fragments(frame(shortfalls=(
        ShortfallRow("b", "near", 3, "cap", True, None, 12),
        ShortfallRow("b", "mid", 2, "rig", False, None, 15),
        ShortfallRow("b", "far", 1, "cleared", False, Box("jake"), 20),
    )), view())["rt-need"]

    assert ("3 short in b · possible avoidance: its failed repeats in a row reached its "
            "limit") in need
    assert ("2 short in b · its repeat limit was reached with tries the rig lost (a lost tracker "
            "or a fault): not flagged as avoidance") in need
    assert ('1 short in b · cleared by <span class="who-b">jake <span class="nm">(box, unverified)'
            in need)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("drumming", "said", "offered"),
    [(None, "not drumming", ["block", "run"]), ("block", "drumming this block", ["run", "off"]),
     ("run", "drumming this run", ["block", "off"])],
)
def test_still_needed_says_whether_trials_are_drummed_and_offers_the_other_switches(
        drumming, said, offered):
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: drumming switched on per block or run by the trainer."""
    need = fragments(frame(drumming=drumming), view())["rt-need"]

    assert f'<span class="nm">{said}</span>' in need
    assert re.findall(r'data-cmd="drum" data-scope="(\w+)"', need) == offered


def test_clear_is_offered_only_while_the_training_aid_is_on_beside_the_training_switches():
    """The PI, 2026-10-18: *clear* is a training aid, off by default, and offered only while it is
    on; a switch turns it on or off, and another sets how a percentage counts drummed answers
    (Task 14's `link.Training`)."""
    off = fragments(frame(owed={"near": 2}), view())["rt-need"]
    on = fragments(frame(owed={"near": 2}, clearing=True, first_answers=True), view())["rt-need"]

    assert 'data-cmd="clear"' not in off
    assert ('<span class="nm">clear is off</span><button type="button" class="btn" '
            'data-cmd="training" data-setting="clear" data-on="1">switch clear on</button>') in off
    assert ('<span class="nm">a percentage counts every answer, drummed too</span>'
            '<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="training" data-setting="first_answers" '
            'data-on="1">count first answers only</button>') in off
    assert 'data-cmd="clear" data-condition="near"' in on
    assert 'data-setting="clear" data-on="0">switch clear off' in on
    assert '<span class="nm">a percentage counts first answers only</span>' in on
    assert 'data-setting="first_answers" data-on="0">count every answer' in on


def test_still_needed_shows_each_conditions_counts():
    """The PI, 2026-10-18: per condition, "trials and presentations; right, wrong, and percent right
    first time; owed, repeats placed, and moved past, with the possible-avoidance flag; drummed
    tries", and the failed run of its trial being drummed now. A condition with no trial yet has no
    percentage."""
    need = fragments(frame(owed={"near": 2, "far": 0}, conditions=(
        ConditionRow("near", 12, 15, 9, 4, 8, 2, 3, 1, True, 3, 2),
        ConditionRow("far", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, False, 0, 0))), view())["rt-need"]

    assert ('<div class="kv"><span class="nm">12 trials · 15 presentations · 9 right · 4 wrong · '
            '67% right first time · 3 repeats · 1 moved past (possible avoidance) · 3 drummed · '
            'failing 2 in a row</span></div>') in need
    assert ('<span class="nm">0 trials · 0 presentations · 0 right · 0 wrong · — right first time '
            '· 0 repeats · 0 moved past · 0 drummed</span>') in need


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("kind", "tone", "said"), [
    ("training", "info", "drumming and clear on: the record marks every trial"),
    ("recording", "warn", "drumming and clear on in a recording session, though training aids are "
                          "meant for training sessions: the record marks every trial"),
])
def test_a_standing_warning_says_a_training_aid_is_on_in_the_words_for_the_sessions_kind(
        kind, tone, said):
    """The PI, 2026-10-18: "a warning that training aids are on in the console", and in a piloting
    or recording session "a standing warning that a training aid is on in a non-training session".
    None while no aid is on, nor once the run has ended."""
    aids = ("drumming", "clear")
    on = fragments(frame(drumming="run", clearing=True, aids=aids, session_kind=kind),
                   view())["banners"]
    ended = fragments(frame(drumming="run", clearing=True, aids=aids, stop_kind="completed",
                            stopped_because="every block is finished"), view())["banners"]

    assert (f'<div class="banner {tone}"><span class="tag">Training aid</span><span>{said}'
            f'</span></div>') in on
    assert "Training aid" not in fragments(frame(), view())["banners"]
    assert "Training aid" not in ended


def test_the_strip_shows_right_first_time_beside_right_per_presentation_where_trials_were_drummed():
    """The PI, 2026-10-18, "Show both": "right first time (per trial, a trial and its drummed
    repeats one) and right per presentation (every try)". A level with drummed tries leads with
    right first time; one with none shows its one figure, as before."""
    perf = replace(frame().performance, run=Counts({"correct": 12, "wrong_target": 8}, 0,
                                                    drummed=6, right_drummed=4))
    strip = fragments(replace(frame(), performance=perf), view())["strip"]

    assert ('8<span class="u"> / 14</span></span><span class="n">57%</span>'
            '<span class="x">run 3 · right first time; 60% per presentation (12 / 20)</span>'
            ) in strip
    assert '30<span class="u"> / 40</span>' in strip, "the session's line, nothing drummed"


def test_working_counts_trials_with_the_presentations_beside_where_drumming_made_them_differ():
    """The PI, 2026-10-18: "The console would show both: trials (200) and presentations (e.g.
    260)" -- a trial and its drummed repeats are one trial (the controller's ruling I1). What
    counts presentations says so: the ticks, which list lines; the strip's rate; and the
    scheduled stop, which counts presentations, drummed tries included, as the PI kept it."""
    parts = fragments(frame(trials=20, trial_index=25), view(trials_per_min=12.0))
    plain = fragments(frame(), view())
    document = page(plain, stale_after_s=30.0, nonce="n0nce", can_write=True)

    assert ('<span class="big">20</span><span class="unit">trials · 25 presentations</span>'
            in parts["rt-work"])
    assert '<span class="big">40</span><span class="unit">trials</span>' in plain["rt-work"]
    assert "the last 3 of 25 presentations, oldest first" in parts["rt-trials"]
    assert "12.0 presentations/min" in parts["strip"]
    assert ('<option value="trials">after N more presentations, drummed tries included</option>'
            in document)


def test_the_pane_says_where_a_switch_changes_nothing_and_a_block_is_past_its_targets():
    """The controller's ruling M4: with *clear* on where nothing in the run can be cleared, the
    pane says so, and the first-answers switch, in a block whose rule is a streak, says it changes
    nothing there; and past a criterion block's targets it says it draws until the criterion is met
    (the smaller ruling)."""
    flat = fragments(frame(owed={"session": 30}, clearable=False, clearing=True), view())["rt-need"]
    streak = fragments(frame(streak=True), view())["rt-need"]
    past = fragments(frame(owed={"near": 0}, past_targets=True), view())["rt-need"]

    assert '<span class="nm">clear is on, but nothing in this run can be cleared</span>' in flat
    assert ('<span class="nm">a percentage counts every answer, drummed too -- a streak counts '
            'every try, so this changes nothing in this block</span>') in streak
```

```python
def test_a_member_clears_a_trial_and_switches_drumming_and_each_reaches_the_rig_as_theirs(rig):
    """Engine build C2a: *clear one* and *clear the rest* on a condition of the Still-needed pane
    send that condition, a drumming switch its scope, and a training switch its setting, each as
    the signed-in member's (the PI, 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-18; the rest, the science review's D5).
    *Clear* is offered while its training aid is on."""
    rig.hub.offer(frame(clearing=True))
    page = rig.browser_page()
    page.click("#signin")
    page.wait_for_selector(SIGNED_IN, timeout=10_000)

    page.click('[data-cmd="clear"][data-condition="ecc 10"]:not([data-rest])')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: rig.dispatch.seen)
    page.click('[data-cmd="clear"][data-condition="ecc 10"][data-rest]')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: len(rig.dispatch.seen) == 2)
    page.click('[data-cmd="drum"][data-scope="run"]')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: len(rig.dispatch.seen) == 3)
    page.click('[data-cmd="training"][data-setting="first_answers"]')
    rig.wait_for(lambda: len(rig.dispatch.seen) == 4)

    one, rest, drummed, setting = rig.dispatch.seen
    member = "Jake Westerberg"
    assert (type(one), one.condition, one.rest, one.by.name) == (ClearOwed, "ecc 10", False, member)
    assert (type(rest), rest.condition, rest.rest, rest.by.name) == (
        ClearOwed, "ecc 10", True, member)
    assert (type(drummed), drummed.scope, drummed.by.name) == (Drum, "run", member)
    assert (type(setting), setting.setting, setting.on, setting.by.name) == (
        Training, "first_answers", True, member)
```

```python
def test_the_strips_counts_carry_each_levels_drummed_tries(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-18, "Show both": the strip's counts at the session, the task, the run and the
    block carry their drummed tries and the right ones among them (telemetry schema 16), so the
    page shows right first time beside right per presentation."""
    block = Block(name="drill", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=20)], drum=True)
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]))
    seen = []
    session.observe = lambda *_: seen.append(session.performance)

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    drummed = sum(row["drummed"] for row in rows)
    right = sum(row["drummed"] and row["outcome"] == "correct" for row in rows)
    last = seen[-1]
    assert drummed > 0 and (last.run.drummed, last.run.right_drummed) == (drummed, right)
    assert (last.session.drummed, last.task.drummed, last.block.drummed) == (drummed,) * 3


def test_a_drummed_run_of_20_trials_shows_20_trials_and_its_presentations_beside(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-18, "200 trials; drummed repeats inside them" -- "The console would show
    both: trials (200) and presentations (e.g. 260)" (the controller's ruling I1): a plain run of
    20 trials, drummed from its start, is 20 trials and more presentations, on the page and on
    /health, through the wire."""
    from _frames import ENDPOINT, view

    from wl_xcon.health import readings
    from wl_xcon.link import decode, encode
    from wl_xcon.web import fragments

    link = _Drained({1: [Drum(scope="run", by=Box("jake"))]})
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=20), link=link)

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    last = decode(encode(link.published[-1]))
    work = fragments(last, view())["rt-work"]
    values = {row["key"]: row["value"] for row in readings(
        last, frame_age_s=1.0, stale_after_s=30.0, rejected=None, endpoint=ENDPOINT)}
    assert len(rows) > 20 and (last.trials, last.trial_index) == (20, len(rows))
    assert (f'<span class="big">20</span><span class="unit">trials · {len(rows)} presentations'
            f'</span>') in work
    assert (values["trials"], values["presentations"]) == ("20", str(len(rows)))
```

```python
    @property
    def clearable(self) -> bool:
        """Whether a trial of the open block can be owed, so a person may clear one from the
        console (the engine C2a plan's engineering review, Minor 3): trials are drummed, or the
        block's table -- or its structure's -- owes some outcome, before its stimulus or after. The
        flat run's every-trial table owes none, so a plain "run N trials" offers no *clear*, where
        one would only shorten the run."""
        if self.drumming:
            return True
        table = self._table
        if table is None:
            return True
        return OWED in {table.of(o, shown) for o in (*Outcome, None) for shown in (True, False)}
```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ShortfallRow:
    """One shortfall on a frame (engine spec §16.6; `scheduler.Shortfall`): trials of a condition's
    debt forgiven in a block, `because` its repeat limit was reached -- by the animal's failures
    (`possible_avoidance`) or with the rig's among them -- or a person cleared them (`by`), after
    trial `after_trial` of the run, or before its first (`None`)."""

    block: str
    condition: str
    trials: int
    because: str
    possible_avoidance: bool
    by: Actor | None
    after_trial: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ConditionRow:
    """One condition of the open block on a frame (`scheduler.ConditionReport`, its block the
    frame's; the PI, 2026-10-18): its trials, a trial and its drummed repeats one, and
    presentations; its right and wrong answers and the trials right first time; what it owes, its
    repeats, the trials moved past and whether its limit flagged it as possible avoidance; its
    drummed tries and the failed tries in a row of its trial being drummed now."""

    condition: str
    trials: int
    presentations: int
    right: int
    wrong: int
    right_first: int
    owed: int
    repeats: int
    moved_past: int
    possible_avoidance: bool
    drummed: int
    failing: int
```

```python
def _need(frame: Telemetry | None) -> str:
    """Still needed, by condition: `scheduler.owed` as published."""
    if frame is None:
        return _NONE
    if not frame.owed:
        return '<span class="nm">nothing still needed</span>'
    return "".join(
        f'<div class="kv"><span class="mono">{_e(condition)}</span>'
        f'<span class="num">{_e(count)}</span></div>'
        for condition, count in frame.owed.items()
    )
```

with

```python
#: The drumming switches, each scope's label (`scheduler.DRUM_SCOPES`; the PI, 2026-10-11).
DRUM_SWITCHES = (("block", "drum this block"), ("run", "drum this run"), ("off", "stop drumming"))

#: Why a repeat limit forgave a condition's debt, as the pane says it (engine spec §16.6).
_FORGIVEN = {
    "cap": "possible avoidance: its failed repeats in a row reached its limit",
    "rig": ("its repeat limit was reached with tries the rig lost (a lost tracker or a fault): not "
            "flagged as avoidance"),
}


def _counted(row: ConditionRow) -> str:
    """One condition's counts (the PI, 2026-10-18): its trials, a trial and its drummed repeats
    one, and presentations; right and wrong answers and percent right first time; repeats placed;
    trials moved past, with the possible-avoidance flag; drummed tries; and the failed run of its
    trial being drummed now. What it owes is on its line above."""
    first = f"{100.0 * row.right_first / row.trials:.0f}%" if row.trials else "—"
    moved = f"{row.moved_past} moved past" + (" (possible avoidance)" if row.possible_avoidance
                                              else "")
    said = [f"{row.trials} trials", f"{row.presentations} presentations", f"{row.right} right",
            f"{row.wrong} wrong", f"{first} right first time", f"{row.repeats} repeats", moved,
            f"{row.drummed} drummed"]
    if row.failing:
        said.append(f"failing {row.failing} in a row")
    return f'<div class="kv"><span class="nm">{_e(" · ".join(said))}</span></div>'


def _need(frame: Telemetry | None, view: View) -> str:
    """Still needed (engine spec §16.6): every condition of the open block with what it still owes
    (`scheduler.owed`) and, beneath it, its counts (`_counted`); beside each owing any, while a run
    runs, the training aid *clear* is on (`clearing`; the PI, 2026-10-18) and a trial of the block
    can be owed (`clearable`), *clear one* (the PI, 2026-10-11: "there should be some way in the
    console to manually advance beyond or clear a specific trial in the count") and, owing more
    than one, *clear the rest* (the engine C2a plan's science review, D5); then the run's
    shortfalls, a repeat limit's flagged as possible avoidance or named for the rig, a clear's with
    who cleared it; then, while a run runs, drumming and its switches (the PI, 2026-10-11), and the
    training switches: *clear* on or off, and how a percentage counts drummed answers."""
    if frame is None:
        return _NONE
    live = frame.phase == "running" and frame.stop_kind is None
    counted = {row.condition: row for row in frame.conditions}

    def clears(condition: str, count: int) -> str:
        if not (live and frame.clearing and frame.clearable and count):
            return ""
        name, off = _e(condition), _gate(view)
        one = (f'<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="clear" data-condition="{name}"{off}>'
               f"clear one</button>")
        rest = (f'<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="clear" data-condition="{name}" '
                f'data-rest="1"{off}>clear the rest</button>')
        return one + (rest if count > 1 else "")

    rows = []
    for condition, count in frame.owed.items():
        rows.append(f'<div class="kv"><span class="mono">{_e(condition)}</span>'
                    f'<span class="num">{_e(count)}</span>{clears(condition, count)}</div>')
        if condition in counted:
            rows.append(_counted(counted[condition]))
    rows = rows or ['<span class="nm">nothing still needed</span>']
    if frame.past_targets:
        # Past a criterion block's targets nothing is owed, and every condition comes until the
        # criterion is met (the PI, 2026-10-18; the controller's smaller ruling).
        rows.append('<div class="kv"><span class="nm">every target used up: drawing until the '
                    'criterion is met</span></div>')
    for short in frame.shortfalls:
        why = (f"cleared by {_who(short.by)}" if short.because == "cleared"
               else _FORGIVEN.get(short.because, _e(short.because)))
        rows.append(
            f'<div class="kv"><span class="mono">{_e(short.condition)}</span>'
            f'<span class="nm">{_e(short.trials)} short in {_e(short.block)} · {why}</span></div>'
        )
    if live:
        now = {"block": "drumming this block", "run": "drumming this run"}.get(
            frame.drumming, "not drumming")
        switches = "".join(
            f'<button type="button" class="btn" data-cmd="drum" data-scope="{scope}"{_gate(view)}>'
            f"{label}</button>"
            for scope, label in DRUM_SWITCHES
            if scope != (frame.drumming or "off")
        )
        rows.append(f'<div class="kv"><span class="nm">{now}</span>{switches}</div>')
        if frame.clearing:
            # The controller's ruling M4: on where nothing in the run can be cleared, it says so.
            said = ("clear is on" if frame.clearable
                    else "clear is on, but nothing in this run can be cleared")
            clear = (said, "0", "switch clear off")
        else:
            clear = ("clear is off", "1", "switch clear on")
        judged = (("a percentage counts first answers only", "0", "count every answer")
                  if frame.first_answers
                  else ("a percentage counts every answer, drummed too", "1",
                        "count first answers only"))
        if frame.streak:
            # The controller's ruling M4: a streak judges every try whatever the setting.
            judged = (f"{judged[0]} -- a streak counts every try, so this changes nothing in this "
                      f"block", *judged[1:])
        for setting, (said, on, label) in (("clear", clear), ("first_answers", judged)):
            rows.append(
                f'<div class="kv"><span class="nm">{said}</span><button type="button" class="btn" '
                f'data-cmd="training" data-setting="{setting}" data-on="{on}"{_gate(view)}>'
                f"{label}</button></div>"
            )
    return "".join(rows)
```

```python
def _aids_banner(frame: Telemetry) -> str:
    """The standing warning while a training aid is on and the run goes (the PI, 2026-10-18: "a
    warning that training aids are on in the console"; outside a training session, "Allow it, with
    a warning"): drumming, a block's or a trainer's, and *clear*, as the scheduler lists them
    (`Telemetry.aids`, never recomputed here: the controller's smaller ruling). Every trial's row

        return ""
    aids = " and ".join(on)
    if frame.session_kind == "training":
        return _banner("info", "Training aid", _e(f"{aids} on: the record marks every trial"))
    return _banner("warn", "Training aid", _e(
        f"{aids} on in a {frame.session_kind} session, though training aids are meant for "
        f"training sessions: the record marks every trial"))
```

### Task 10: A hold runs its own time, leaving the target early is a target break, and a miss steps the staircase up

Three changes to the reference tasks' behavior, each one an answer, and a load-time warning beside the first.

**A hold in progress is never cut off by a timeout** (the controller's ruling on the science review's O2). The four detection and search tasks raced `Hold(target_hold)` against `After(response_deadline)` in `verify`, both from the state's entry, and the ranges cross (a hold up to 1 s, a deadline from 0.1 s): raising `target_hold` past `response_deadline`, the ordinary way to shape a hold, ended every held target as `NO_RESPONSE` -- a miss, counted and unrewarded, in the detection tasks (the PI's decision 6), spent in `visual_search`. The deadline now bounds the response only, in the state that waits for it (`stim_on`, `array_on`); the hold runs its own time, and `verify`'s bound is a backstop check 4 needs, 3 s: longer than any legal `target_hold`, and no longer than the deadline's own highest, so no trial lasts longer than it could before. **The same defect stood in each task's fixation hold**, which the load-time warning below finds: `hold_fix` raced `Hold(fix_hold)` (up to 2 s) against `After(fix_acquisition)` (from 0.5 s), so a fixation hold set past the acquisition time was cut off as `NO_FIXATION`, and `calibration`'s `hold` did the same with `cal_hold`. Each gets the same kind of backstop, 10 s, `fix_acquisition`'s highest (call 17). At the tasks' starting values nothing changes: every hold is shorter than its old bound.

**The controller's pre-flight ruling (2026-10-18), with correction 3 in the header.** Because `hold-cut-short` reads through `_span`, this task also: (a) adds `"hold-cut-short"` after `"outside-range"` in the expected findings of `below` in `tests/test_range_checks.py`'s `test_a_wait_with_no_highest_makes_no_wait_longer_than_any_the_task_declares` (a timeout declared with a lowest of 1.0 only, sent at 0.5, can now end before the hold: a true warning), with a sentence in its docstring saying so; (b) adds two tests beside this task's `hold-cut-short` tests, written the plan's way: a `Hold` whose setting declares no highest is warned against a plain timeout, its sentence reading "with no longest declared"; and a timeout whose setting declares only a lowest, below the hold's highest, is warned. Each is seen failing under a named break (the span reading only two-bounded settings, as `_ranges` did), restored with `cmp`.

**A load-time warning, `hold-cut-short`**, for any state whose plain `After` can fire before its `Hold` can complete, within their declared ranges: a warning, accepted in every session kind (the PI's rule of 2026-10-11, warnings over refusals), saying which state, which hold and which bound, and at what settings. A bound naming the hold's own setting is not one. The warnings build's `beyond_range` re-runs the checks with a range widened to a value outside it, so a hold set past its range can raise this warning there too; its test of `fix_hold` at 8 s, whose state's `fix_acquisition` timeout starts at 0.5 s, now expects it beside `outside-range` (call 18).

**Leaving the target before its hold is complete is a target break in both detection tasks** (the PI, 2026-10-13, answering Appendix B item 6, "Accept, target break": "Take the plan's tables, make leaving the target early a target break in the two detection tasks"). It was `WRONG_TARGET`, a distractor-type answer in a task that shows no distractor; now `TARGET_BREAK`, strobing `OUTCOME_TARGET_BREAK` (4120) as `visual_search` does, so the stream says why the trial aborted. Their tables already show a break after the target again (items 5 and 7), and a target break is no answer, so it does not move `adaptive_detection`'s staircase (`next_params` reads correct, wrong and missed answers only; `test_an_aborted_trial_does_not_move_the_staircase` already holds a target break to that). Nothing else in the two tasks keys on `WRONG_TARGET`: their reaction time ends at `verify`, they offer no choice, and the allocation marks a target break as `TRIAL_ABORT` with its reason.

**A miss steps `adaptive_detection`'s staircase up** (the PI, 2026-10-12, "Yes, a miss steps contrast up": "A miss counts as an incorrect answer, so after misses the target gets more visible"). `next_params` moved the staircase on a correct answer and a wrong one only, so with misses the PI's decision 6 made answers, a target below threshold stepped contrast down on corrects and never back up (the science review's O3); TSK-33's default already counted a miss a failure. TSK-34 records his answer (Task 11).

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/check.py` (`_holds_cut_short` before `_target_name`; `check` calls it)
- Modify: `tasks/fixation_detection.py`, `tasks/adaptive_detection.py` (`verify`'s target break), `tasks/fixation_detection.py`, `tasks/adaptive_detection.py`, `tasks/visual_search.py`, `tasks/visual_search_training.py` (`hold_fix`'s and `verify`'s bounds), `tasks/calibration.py` (`hold`'s bound), `tasks/adaptive_detection.py` (`next_params`)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_range_checks.py` (one new; the warnings build's in-range wait test expects the new warning), `tests/test_reference_tasks.py` (imports; four new; one changed)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `check._span`, `_is_number`, `_target_name` (unchanged).
- Produces: `check`'s finding `hold-cut-short` (a warning, every session kind); `TARGET_BREAK` (with `Mark(4120)`) where the detection tasks ended a broken target hold as `WRONG_TARGET`; `next_params` moving the staircase on `CORRECT`, `WRONG_TARGET` and `NO_RESPONSE`.

`tests/test_range_checks.py`: insert

```python
def test_an_after_that_can_beat_a_hold_on_its_state_is_a_warning():
    """The engine C2a plan's science review, O2: `verify` raced `Hold(target_hold)` against
    `After(response_deadline)`, both from the state's entry, and their ranges crossed, so a hold set
    past the deadline was cut off and scored a miss. A warning, accepted in every session kind (the
    PI, 2026-10-11: warnings over refusals); a bound longer than the longest hold, or the hold's own
    setting, is not one."""
    def verify(bound) -> Trial:
        return Trial(
            start="verify",
            params=[Param("target_hold", unit="s", low=0.05, high=1.0),
                    Param("response_deadline", unit="s", low=0.1, high=3.0)],
            windows=[Window("target", at=(0.0, 0.0), radius=2.0, on="fix")],
            states=[State("verify", enter=[Show(FIX)], go=[
                On(Hold("target", P("target_hold")), Outcome.CORRECT),
                On(After(bound), Outcome.NO_RESPONSE),
            ])],
        )

    (cut,) = [f for f in check(verify(P("response_deadline"))) if f.code == "hold-cut-short"]
    assert cut.is_warning and not cut.refuses("recording")
    assert "'verify'" in cut.detail and "NO_RESPONSE" in cut.detail
    assert "as soon as 0.1 s" in cut.detail and "up to 1 s" in cut.detail
    for bound in (3.0, P("target_hold")):
        assert "hold-cut-short" not in codes(verify(bound))
```

```python
def test_a_miss_steps_the_staircases_contrast_up():
    """The PI, 2026-10-12, "Yes, a miss steps contrast up": "A miss counts as an incorrect answer,
    so after misses the target gets more visible." It moved nothing, so below threshold the
    staircase stepped down on corrects and never back up (the engine C2a plan's science review,
    O3)."""
    module_staircase = _load("adaptive_detection", "Staircase")
    next_params = _load("adaptive_detection", "next_params")
    staircase = module_staircase(value=0.5)

    next_params(staircase, target_eccentricity=10.0, last=Outcome.NO_RESPONSE)

    assert staircase.value > 0.5
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", sorted(TASK_FILES))
def test_no_reference_task_cuts_a_hold_short(name):
    """The engine C2a plan (its science review, O2): no `After` can end a reference task's state
    while a hold there may still be running, at any legal setting -- the fixation hold, the target
    hold and calibration's -- so `hold-cut-short` finds nothing."""
    task = _load_task(TASKS / f"{name}.py")

    assert "hold-cut-short" not in {f.code for f in check_task(task)}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["fixation_detection", "adaptive_detection"])
def test_a_target_held_past_the_response_deadline_is_correct(name):
    """The science review's O2: with `target_hold` set past `response_deadline` -- how a trainer
    shapes a longer hold -- a target held to the end was scored `NO_RESPONSE`, a miss, because the
    deadline ran from `verify`'s entry too. The deadline bounds the response; the hold runs its
    own time. Gaze holds the fixation point, lands on the target at frame 45 and stays: the old
    bound, 0.3 s from `verify`'s entry, ended it at frame 75; the hold's 0.9 s runs past it."""
    trial = _load_task(TASKS / f"{name}.py").trial
    world = Scripted({SaccadeTo("target"): 45},
                     inside={f: "fix" if f <= 40 else "target" for f in range(1, 400)})
    values = {**VALUES, "response_deadline": 0.3, "target_hold": 0.9}

    result = run_trial(trial, world, 0.01, values=values, effects=Recorded())

    assert result.outcome is Outcome.CORRECT and result.frames >= 45 + 90


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["fixation_detection", "adaptive_detection"])
def test_leaving_the_target_before_its_hold_is_a_target_break(name):
    """The PI, 2026-10-13, answering the engine C2a plan's Appendix B item 6 ("Accept, target
    break"): in a task that shows no distractor, leaving the target before its hold is complete is
    a criterion not kept, never a wrong answer -- a target break, shown again by its table and
    moving no staircase -- and the task strobes why (`OUTCOME_TARGET_BREAK`, 4120). Gaze lands on
    the target at frame 45 and leaves it at frame 60, before its 0.2 s hold."""
    trial = _load_task(TASKS / f"{name}.py").trial
    world = Scripted({SaccadeTo("target"): 45},
                     inside={f: "fix" if f <= 40 else "target" for f in range(1, 60)})
    effects = Recorded()

    result = run_trial(trial, world, 0.01, values=VALUES, effects=effects)

    assert result.outcome is Outcome.TARGET_BREAK and ("mark", 4120) in effects.log
```

`wl_xcon/check.py`: insert

```python
def _holds_cut_short(trial: Trial) -> list[Finding]:
    """A `Hold` that an `After` on the same state can beat, within their declared ranges, is a hold
    cut off in progress: the trial ends on the timeout, scored as whatever it leads to, while the
    animal is still holding (the engine C2a plan's science review, O2: a held target scored a miss
    when `target_hold` was set past `response_deadline`). **A warning, never a refusal** (the PI,
    2026-10-11: warnings over refusals), naming the state, the hold, the bound and their settings.
    Only a plain `After` is compared -- one with a `since` starts later -- and a bound naming the
    hold's own setting cannot beat it. Each setting is read through `_span`, as every range check
    is: a side its range does not bound reaches as far as that side can go, so a hold with no
    highest can be cut short by any timeout, and a timeout with no lowest can end at once."""
    params = {p.name: p for p in trial.params}

    def span(value) -> "tuple[float, float] | None":
        if isinstance(value, P) or _is_number(value):
            return _span(value, params, 0.0)
        return None

    def said(value) -> str:
        return f"`{value.name}`" if isinstance(value, P) else f"{value:g} s"

    findings: list[Finding] = []
    for state in trial.states:
        holds = [edge for edge in state.go if isinstance(edge.guard, Hold)]
        bounds = [edge for edge in state.go
                  if isinstance(edge.guard, After) and edge.guard.since is None]
        for hold in holds:
            for bound in bounds:
                held, waits = span(hold.guard.seconds), span(bound.guard.seconds)
                if held is None or waits is None or bound.guard.seconds == hold.guard.seconds:
                    continue
                if waits[0] < held[1]:
                    longest = (f"up to {held[1]:g} s" if math.isfinite(held[1])
                               else "with no longest declared")
                    findings.append(Finding("hold-cut-short", (
                        f"state {state.name!r} ends on its {_target_name(bound.to)} timeout after "
                        f"{said(bound.guard.seconds)}, as soon as {waits[0]:g} s, while its "
                        f"{hold.guard.window!r} hold takes {said(hold.guard.seconds)}, "
                        f"{longest}: an animal still holding is cut off and scored "
                        f"{_target_name(bound.to)}; bound the state by a wait longer than the "
                        f"longest hold"), blocking=False, accepted_in=SESSION_KINDS))
    return findings
```

add

```markdown

**Counting and repeats, since engine build C2a.** Each trial structure declares its counting table
(`task.Counting`; engine spec §16.1-16.2), naming the state its stimulus appears in: every answer
counted; a fixation break, no fixation, a
lost tracker and a fault owed, shown again; a break after the stimulus appears spent or owed as the
task says, with no default (`check.check_task` refuses a structure without a table); no response a
miss where withholding is an answer; a wrong answer under "until N correct" unpaid, never placed as
a repeat; and a repeat limit, none unless a task sets one, counting failed tries in a row of one
trial, which any answer ends (the task's limit holding in every block unless the block sets its
own). A block may count by a table of its own (`scheduler.Block.counting`). The scheduler places a
repeat at a free random slot within the next k trials, k at least 3, never the very next, unless
nothing else can be drawn (§16.3); judges every answer for a block's rule, and only answers -- a
streak (a proportion of 1.0) every try in order, a percentage every try unless a trainer sets it
to first answers only; ends a criterion block once its criterion is met and every target is used
up, drawing every condition past them; drums a block or a run when asked (every trial that is not
right back at once until it is, a console warning after ten failed tries in a row); and forgives a
condition's debt past its limit -- flagged as possible avoidance only when the animal's own failures
reached it -- or when a person clears one trial of it, or the rest, from the console. **Drumming
and *clear* are training aids** (the PI, 2026-10-18): off until a block declares drumming or a
trainer switches either on, with a standing warning on the console while one is on, worded for the
session's kind. Each trial's row records what it did to the count, whether its stimulus appeared,
the trial it repeats, the condition before it and the aids on; each run's end row records the
shortfalls and each condition's counts, a trial and its drummed repeats one trial. A trial
that reaches the longest trial, five minutes at the session's rate (`run.LONGEST_TRIAL_S`), is a
missed acquisition, owed, and said on the console. A hold runs its own time: the load-time check
warns where a timeout on its state can cut it short (`hold-cut-short`).
```

```markdown
*Engine build C2a, YYYY-MM-DD* (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-engine-c2a.md`; the PI's
decisions of 2026-10-11, his answers of 2026-10-12 and his confirmation of the plan's Appendix B):
**items 1 and 2** -- each trial structure declares its table (`task.Counting`): answers counted
(correct rejections and false alarms among them); a fixation break, no fixation, a lost tracker and
a fault owed; a break after the stimulus appears spent or owed as the task says, **with no
default**, `check.check_task` refusing a structure without a table; no response a miss, counted,
where the task says withholding is an answer (both detection tasks, the PI); and per outcome
otherwise where it says so, a wrong answer under "until N correct" unpaid and never placed as a
repeat, as the 2026-08-31 rule above has it. The stimulus has appeared once the trial entered the
state its table names (`Counting.appears`; the PI, 2026-10-13: "Task names it, required", a cued
task naming its cue), which the load-time check requires; a break before that is owed. A block may
count by a table of its
own (`Block.counting`: `CORRECT_ONLY`, `PRESENTED`, `EVERY_TRIAL`, the flat run's). **Item 3** --
"later" is the same for every order type and end rule (the PI, 2026-10-12: "Same rule, within the
next k" -- "One rule for every block. Repeats don't pile up at the block's end."): a random slot
within the next k trials, k the block's number of conditions and at least 3 (the PI, 2026-10-12:
"At least 3" -- "A repeat lands 2 or 3 trials later even in a two-condition block, so its position
is not fixed"), never the very next, a slot no other waiting repeat holds; the repeat's condition is
not drawn otherwise while it waits; when nothing else can be drawn the earliest waiting repeat comes
now, at once when it is the trial just broken, which the row records. **Drumming** (the PI): a
block, or a trainer from the console for a block or a run, brings every trial that is not right --
a correct answer or a correct rejection -- straight back until it is, and after 10 failed tries in
a row of one trial the console warns, drumming going on (the PI, 2026-10-12: "After 10 in a row").
**Judging, as the PI defined it on 2026-10-18**: a block's rule judges answers only -- right or
wrong, a miss among them where the task makes no response an answer -- and every answer, whatever
the table says about paying ("2: only answers make or break a streak"; "Yes, every answer counts in
the 20"); under drumming a streak (a rule of proportion 1.0) judges every answer in order ("Every
answer counts in order"), and a percentage does too unless the training setting "first answers
only" is on ("Every answer"), within which his answers of 2026-10-12 ("Leave drummed repeats out")
and 2026-10-18 ("Judge its first answer") hold; a criterion block ends only once its criterion is
met and every target is used up, drawing every condition past them ("Both: criterion and every
target"), and no drum waits ("Finish the drum first"); a block whose own table counts a broken hold
as paying -- a hold-training block -- has it judged a miss ("Yes, where the block says so"); a
trial and its drummed repeats are one trial ("200 trials; drummed repeats inside them"), and the
console counts trials with the presentations beside them. **Drumming and *clear* are training aids** ("Drumming and 'clear'"): off unless a block
declares drumming or a trainer switches either on, a standing warning on the console while one is
on, in any kind of session ("Allow it, with a warning"), and every trial's row marking them; per-
animal training profiles to load them are XC-379. **Item 4**
-- each trial's row records the condition before it in its block, whether its stimulus appeared,
the trial it repeats and whether it came at once; the repeat rule of blocks whose order is the
design waits for those orders (C3, XC-361). **Item 5** waits for drawn values (C3, XC-362).
**Item 6, amended** (the PI, 2026-10-12): a task's repeat limit counts "Tries in a row of one trial"
-- "The condition is moved past after 3 failed repeats in a row, and the count resets once it is
done" -- and on 2026-10-18 "1: an answer ends the trial": any answer, right or wrong, starts the
count over; **none unless a task sets one** (the PI, 2026-10-11), and the task's limit holds in a
block that counts by its own table unless that table sets one ("No: the task's limit applies unless
the block sets its own"); past it the remaining debt is forgiven, flagged as possible avoidance when
the animal's own outcomes reached it and named for the rig when a lost tracker or a fault was among
the tries; a person clears one trial of a condition's count, or the rest, from the console, once
*clear* is switched on, and *clear one* starts the count over ("End that waiting trial; count
starts over"); all are each run's end row's `shortfalls` and the page's Still-needed pane, beside
each condition's counts (the PI, 2026-10-18).
```

```markdown
- **Revised:** 2026-10-18, the PI, answering the engine C2a plan's counting questions: a rule judges **answers only** -- right (correct, a correct rejection) or wrong (a wrong target, a false alarm, an early or a late response or error, a miss where the task makes no response an answer) -- and a trial with no answer neither adds to nor breaks a streak or a percentage ("2: only answers make or break a streak"); **every answer is judged**, whatever the block's counting says about paying, "until N correct" included ("Yes, every answer counts in the 20"); and **in a block that ends on a criterion each condition's target is a minimum** ("Both: criterion and every target": "The block ends only once the criterion is met and every target is used up; conditions keep coming until both hold"). Then, answering the plan's revision review: **only where a block's own table counts a broken hold as paying -- a hold-training block -- is the break judged, a miss** ("Yes, where the block says so": "Only where a block itself declares a broken hold as a scored trial (a hold-training block) does it count as a miss toward the percentage. Everywhere else, breaks stay out, as you decided."); and **a block ends only once a drum waiting is answered right** ("Finish the drum first": "The block ends only after the drummed trial is answered right. If the extra tries drop the percentage below the bar, the block carries on. Training ends on a success."). That a rule of proportion 1.0 is a streak, that past its targets a criterion block draws every condition by its order, and that `Block.criterion_over` is removed (a judged set named by a block could only contradict the two rules) are engineering, the plan's Appendix B items 14 to 16, which he approved; that a block's own table cannot make no response an answer (the structure decides, as for every answer) is the controller's ruling M8 on that review. Built YYYY-MM-DD by engine build C2a (`Scheduler._answer`, `_scored`, `finished`, `past_targets`); the plan's two backlog lines for what these settle were never filed.
```

`docs/references/decisions.md`: after

```markdown
- **Basis:** PI decision, on published findings. Order as the design: in priming of pop-out the previous trial's features and target position change performance, [@bichot2002priming] (abstract) and [@westerberg2020priming] (Materials and Methods: blocks of constant target color, then a swap); [@brooks2012counterbalancing] — carryover from one condition to the next (abstract). Flagging possible avoidance is the PI's; as an analogy only, monkeys chose an offered, rewarded sure option more when uncertain [@kiani2009representation] (abstract), which does not show animals erring or breaking fixation to avoid a condition. The cap and the stated limitation are the PI's.
```
